# Always needed:
# - Scaled by 0.5, 0.75, 1.0
# - Attrition/no
# - Disband on peace yes/no

# Peasant rabble (peasant revolts, large portions of heretic revolts, religious revolts, some portion of MoH revolts)

spawn_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

spawn_t0_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = {
	spawn_unit = {
		province = event_target:target_province
		home = event_target:target_province
		owner = event_target:army_owner
		scaled_by_biggest_garrison = 0.5
		attrition = 1.0

		troops = {
			# T0: Base city, swap some archers for LI, LC
			light_infantry = { 85 85 }
			archers = { 20 20 }
			light_cavalry = { 10 10 }
		}
	}
}

spawn_t1_peasant_army_scale_05_effect = {
	spawn_unit = {
		province = event_target:target_province
		home = event_target:target_province
		owner = event_target:army_owner
		scaled_by_biggest_garrison = 0.5
		attrition = 1.0

		troops = {
			# T1: T0 + ct_barracks_1, swap some archers for LI, LC
			light_infantry = { 150 150 }
			archers = { 35 35 }
			light_cavalry = { 15 15 }
		}
	}
}

		home = event_target:target_province

		scaled_by_biggest_garrison = 0.5
		attrition = 1.0

		troops = {
			# T2: T1 + ct_barracks_2 + ct_guard_1, swap some archers, pikemen for LI, LC
			light_infantry = { 225 225 }
			archers = { 75 75 }
			light_cavalry = { 25 25 }
			pikemen = { 30 30 }
		}
	}
}

spawn_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_peasant_army_scale_1_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

spawn_peasant_army_scale_05_no_attrition_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_peasant_army_scale_05_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_peasant_army_scale_05_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_peasant_army_scale_05_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_peasant_army_scale_05_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_peasant_army_scale_05_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}

spawn_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {

				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_peasant_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

spawn_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CITY_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_peasant_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Tribal liberation revolts

spawn_tribal_army_scale_05_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_tribal_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_tribal_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_tribal_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_tribal_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_tribal_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_tribal_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_tribal_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}

	event_target:army_owner = {

					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_tribal_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_tribal_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {

					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}

					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_tribal_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_tribal_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {

					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_tribal_army_scale_075_effect = yes

					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_tribal_army_scale_075_effect = yes

				}

spawn_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_tribal_army_scale_05_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_tribal_army_scale_05_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_tribal_army_scale_05_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_tribal_army_scale_05_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_tribal_army_scale_05_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_tribal_army_scale_05_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_tribal_army_scale_05_disbanding_effect = yes

		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_tribal_army_scale_05_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_tribal_army_scale_05_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
	}

spawn_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_tribal_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

spawn_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_tribal_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

		attrition = 1.0

		troops = {

	}

# Steppe nomad liberation/clan revolts

spawn_steppe_army_scale_05_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_steppe_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_steppe_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_steppe_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_steppe_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_steppe_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_steppe_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_steppe_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}

		province = event_target:target_province
		home = event_target:target_province
		owner = event_target:army_owner
		scaled_by_biggest_garrison = 0.5
		attrition = 1.0
		disband_on_peace = yes

		troops = {
			# T1: T0 + no_horse_breeder_1 + no_riding_contests_1 + no_hunting_parties_1 + no_composite_bow_crafter_1
			light_cavalry = { 115 115 }
			knights = { 10 10 }
			horse_archers = { 35 35 }
		}
	}
}

spawn_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

spawn_t0_steppe_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = {
	spawn_unit = {
		province = event_target:target_province
		home = event_target:target_province
		owner = event_target:army_owner
		scaled_by_biggest_garrison = 0.75
		attrition = 1.0
		disband_on_peace = yes

		troops = {
			# T0: Base steppe nomad
			light_cavalry = { 100 100 }
			knights = { 5 5 }
			horse_archers = { 15 15 }
		}
	}
}

# Desert nomad liberation/clan revolts

spawn_desert_army_scale_05_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_desert_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_desert_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_desert_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_desert_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_desert_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_desert_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}

spawn_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_desert_army_scale_075_no_attrition_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# Nationalist/MoH revolts

spawn_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

spawn_t0_nationalist_army_scale_05_effect = {
	spawn_unit = {
		province = event_target:target_province
		home = event_target:target_province
		owner = event_target:army_owner
		scaled_by_biggest_garrison = 0.5
		attrition = 1.0

		troops = {
			# T0: Base castle, swapping some LI, HI to P, A, HC, LC to roughly match vanilla nationalist comp
			pikemen = { 60 60 }
			heavy_infantry = { 30 30 }
			light_infantry = { 25 25 }
			archers = { 50 50 }
			light_cavalry = { 40 40 }
			knights = { 20 20 }
		}
	}
}

spawn_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

spawn_t0_nationalist_army_scale_075_effect = {
	spawn_unit = {
		province = event_target:target_province
		home = event_target:target_province
		owner = event_target:army_owner
		scaled_by_biggest_garrison = 0.75
		attrition = 1.0

		troops = {
			# T0: Base castle, swapping some LI, HI to P, A, HC, LC to roughly match vanilla nationalist comp
			pikemen = { 60 60 }
			heavy_infantry = { 30 30 }
			light_infantry = { 25 25 }
			archers = { 50 50 }
			light_cavalry = { 40 40 }
			knights = { 20 20 }
		}
	}
}

spawn_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_nationalist_army_scale_075_no_attrition_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

spawn_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = {
	event_target:army_owner = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 1
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t0_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 2
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t1_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 3
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t2_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 4
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t3_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 5
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t4_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 6
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t5_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION < 7
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t6_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:target_province = {
					TECH_CASTLE_CONSTRUCTION <8
				}
			}
			
			spawn_t7_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
		else = {
			spawn_t8_nationalist_army_scale_075_disbanding_effect = yes
		}
	}
}
